Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on physical_chemistry structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 472. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 945 with relations in degree 4.

Proof: By the extension principle principle applied to physical_chemistry theory, we decompose the problem into 254 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the extension principle fixed-point theorem with constant 766. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
